**Book 177:** *Buku Panduan Guru Matematika untuk SD/MI Kelas I* (teacher guide) — Indonesia, Dara Retno Wulan and Rasfaniwaty, Kementerian Pendidikan, Kebudayaan, Riset, dan Teknologi — Pusat Perbukuan (Kurikulum Merdeka), 2022, ISBN 978-602-244-875-4. Filed in maths/books/indonesia.md (FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED). Teacher evidence only: the matched companion guide of the Grade 1 student book cited in this lesson — Panduan Umum (Fase A outcomes and ATP, printed pp. 1–14) plus one guide section per student chapter, with objectives, lesson flow and timing, activities, misconception notes, remedial and enrichment tasks, project rubrics, answer keys keyed to named student-book pages, and reproducible ten-frame, number-bond and game templates (printed pp. 1–200). It teaches no pupil content beyond the student book. The guide reproduces the student book's chapter covers, placeholder ISBN and all; its own ISBN is as given here. Guide citation stocked by the 2026-11-08 re-verification sweep.

**Book 178:** *Buku Panduan Guru Matematika untuk SD/MI Kelas II* (teacher guide) — Indonesia, Dara Retno Wulan and Rasfaniwaty, Kementerian Pendidikan, Kebudayaan, Riset, dan Teknologi — Pusat Perbukuan (Kurikulum Merdeka), 2022, ISBN 978-602-427-898-4. Filed in maths/books/indonesia.md (FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED). Teacher evidence only: the matched companion guide of the Grade 2 student book cited in this lesson — general guidance plus one guide section per student chapter, with lesson flow, the two-step word-problem method (known → asked → solution → conclusion) and four keyword cards, misconception handling, parent interaction, assessment rubrics, remedial and enrichment tasks, answer keys and reproducible aids (printed pp. 1–184). It teaches no pupil content beyond the student book. Its Chapter 1 pages (printed pp. 20–42) and Chapter 2 cover (p. 43) misprint the running footer as "Kelas I"; cite the cover and title page. Guide citation stocked by the 2026-11-08 re-verification sweep.

**Book 179:** *Buku Panduan Guru Matematika untuk SD/MI Kelas III* (teacher guide) — Indonesia, Susanto, Arika Indah Kristiana, Arif Fatahillah, Eko Waluyo, Ridho Alfarisi, Hobri, Kementerian Pendidikan, Kebudayaan, Riset, dan Teknologi — Pusat Perbukuan (Kurikulum Merdeka), 2022, ISBN 978-602-427-936-3. **Book 180:** *Buku Panduan Guru Matematika untuk SD/MI Kelas IV* (teacher guide) — Indonesia, Hobri, Susanto, Arika Indah Kristiana, Arif Fatahillah, Eko Waluyo, Ridho Alfarisi, Haris Setiya Budi, Moh. | Column subtraction with borrowing — grade 2 pupils collected 125 bottles and grade 1 pupils 57 bottles: by how many more bottles did the grade 2 pupils collect than the grade 1 pupils? (125 − 57 worked in the place table and column form: first subtract the ones — from 5 ones, 7 ones cannot be taken, so break 1 ten into ones: 1 ten 5 ones = 15 ones; 15 ones − 7 ones = 8 ones; then the tens: 1 ten remained; from 1 hundred … 11 tens − 5 tens = 6 tens; the splitting check: 125 is 110 and 15, 57 is 50 and 7, 60 and 8 join to make 68; answer: 6 tens and 8 ones make 68); do the subtractions (132 − 41; 117 − 59; 142 − 67; 124 − 36; 156 − 74; 163 − 83; 120 − 54; 144 − 68); count down by tens from 196 to 56; count up by tens from 34; count down by tens from 187 to 97; place the tens whose sum is 100 on the vertices of the triangle shapes (30, 50, 20 shown on one); there were 113 sheep and goats in the pen, 48 of them goats — how many sheep were in the pen? | Mongolia, Математик II (Grade 2), printed p. 102 |
| Revision: column subtraction with borrowing — worked example 4: the 2012 prices of some food goods are shown (bread 650₮; 1 kg carrots 955₮; 1 kg flour 757₮; 1 kg cabbage 969₮; 1 kg potatoes 800₮): 1. explain the method of finding the price difference of 1 kg of carrots and 1 kg of cabbage — to find the difference of two numbers, subtract the smaller number from the bigger: a. 969 − 955 = 969 − 900 − 50 − 5 = 69 − 50 − 5 = 19 − 5 = 14; b. in the place table and column steps (subtract the ones first: 9 − 5 = 4; then the tens: 6 − 5 = 1 ten; then the hundreds, giving 014); check: 955 + 14 = 969; 2. find the price differences (1 kg carrots and 1 kg potatoes; 1 kg cabbage and 1 kg potatoes); compose a problem about buying goods and have your friends solve it; using the given template, calculate with a number line: 472 − 49 = ? (jump back by tens and ones: 472, 470, 460 with the split 49 = 9 + 40 shown in boxes); calculate (321 − 42; 545 − 28; 222 − 59; 190 − 82; 604 − 71); mental task: when 100 tögrög was exchanged into tens, it made 12 notes — when it was exchanged into fives and tens, into how many notes of fives and of tens was it exchanged? | Mongolia, Математик III (Grade 3), printed p. 8 |
| Revision: borrowing across zeros, error fixing — worked example 5: explain the method of finding the difference between the prices of 1 kg of carrots and 1 kg of flour: a. 955 − 757 = 955 − 700 − 50 − 7 = 205 − 7 = 190 + 15 − 7 = 190 + 8 = 198; b. with place blocks and column steps: subtract the ones — from 5 ones, 7 ones cannot be taken, so break 1 ten: 1 ten 5 ones = 15 ones, 15 ones − 7 ones = 8 ones; subtract the tens — 1 hundred is broken: 1 hundred = 10 tens, 10 tens + 4 tens = 14 tens, 14 tens − 5 tens = 9 tens; subtract the hundreds: 8 hundreds − 7 hundreds = 1 hundred; check: 198 + 757 = 198 + 2 + 755 = 200 + 755 = 955; find the difference between the prices of 1 kg of potatoes and 1 kg of flour: 800 − 757 = ? explained the same way (ones: from 0, 7 cannot be taken; the hundreds are broken: 1 hundred = 10 tens, 1 ten = 10 ones, 10 ones − 7 ones = 3 ones; tens: 9 tens − 5 tens = 4 tens; hundreds: 7 hundreds remain, 7 − 7 = 0); check: 43 + 757 = 40 + 3 + 700 + 50 + 7 = 700 + 90 + 10 = 800; find the errors, correct them and explain (740 − 63 written as 723; 600 − 349 as 361; 600 − 349 as 949; 503 − 146 as 443 — planted wrong answers; correct values 677, 251, 251, 357) | Mongolia, Математик III (Grade 3), printed p. 9 |
| Column subtraction within 10,000 — by how many tögrög did the pencil case become cheaper? (its price tag is crossed from 850₮ to 630₮: the expression 850 − 630); by how many tögrög did the book become cheaper? (crossed from 6850₮ to 5630₮: write the expression); calculate by splitting the numbers into place addends (850 − 600 − 30 = 250 − 30 = …; 6850 − 5000 − 600 − 30 = 1850 − 600 − … = 1250 − … = …); place the numbers in a place table and then in columns (850 − 630: 8 hundreds − 6 hundreds, 5 tens − 3 tens, 0 − 0; 6850 − 5630: 6 thousand − 5 thousand, 8 − 6 hundreds, 5 − 3 tens, 0 − 0 ones; answer boxes: it became cheaper by … tögrög); calculate (1850 − 630; 6850 − 2630; 8958 − 3615; 4367 − 1355); find the difference's value following the template and complete the column writing (3823 − 1238 with the breaking marks shown: 3823 − 1238 = 2585; a speech asks: subtracting a three-digit number is just the same as the numbers you know — how do you check whether the difference's value is right?); do the operations and check them (eight subtractions within 10,000 that pair with the addition set of printed p. 25, e.g. 3538 − 2365; 4342 − 2358; 6259 − 651; 6780 − 3057; 3400 − 3246); do the operations and check them (5106 − 327 and 5106 + 327; 3279 + 821 and 3279 + 4821; 9521 − 532 and 9521 − 4532; 3000 − 1518; 8000 − 5294) | Mongolia, Математик III (Grade 3), printed p. 26 |
| Counting-up method; planted pupil error; mental compensation — worked example 2: explain the calculations — finding a difference by counting up: 123 − 56 = ?: how much must be added to 56 to make 123? 56 + 4 = 60; 60 + 40 = 100; 100 + 23 = 123; 4 + 40 + 23 = 67, so 123 − 56 = 67; 4328 − 1973 = ?: how much must be added to 1973 to make 4328? 1973 + 7 = 1980; 1980 + 20 = 2000; 2000 + 2328 = 4328; 2328 + 20 + 7 = 2355, so 4328 − 1973 = 2355; calculate and check whether the correct answer came out (413 − 254; 1165 − 537; 607 − 326; 9136 − 3864; 1204 − 835; 8103 − 2860; 2139 − 720; 9000 − 5699); explain the girl's mistake in her calculation (her column subtraction 7050 − 3432 is written with the answer 4618 and her speech says "I forgot to break up a thousand: 7 thousand − 3 thousand = 4 thousand, so I wrote 4 in the difference's thousands place" — a planted pupil error; the correct difference is 3618); is the other calculation right (the column addition 3606 + 295 written as 3801)? Why? (it is also wrong — the correct sum is 3901); write the numbers in your notebook and connect the numbers whose sum is 1000 (738, 456, 270, 730, 905, 262, 544, 5); find the numbers whose sum is 10000 (3505, 2900, 2385, 7100, 5050, 7615, 4950, 6495); observe the calculation and calculate mentally (by compensation: 3245 + 697 = 3245 + 700 − 3 = 3945 − 3 = 3942 — correct as printed; the second worked example as printed reads "4719 − 2199 = 4719 − 2200 + 1 = 6919 + 1 = 6920", which is arithmetically wrong — 4719 − 2200 = 2519, so the chain should end 2519 + 1 = 2520; flagged as a suspected book error, see note); then: 576 + 299; 580 − 299; 564 + 198; 971 − 498; 841 + 998; 657 − 386; 1580 + 2900; 6925 − 3299; 4189 + 3498; 8518 − 4975; write the prices of the goods before and after the price cut (₮ is tögrög: gloves 9999₮ → 6199₮; nuts 8199₮ → 6599₮; a board 2500₮ → 1990₮; a food container 7900₮ → 4990₮): by how much did each good become cheaper? Which good became cheapest by the most? With 10000 tögrög, which of these goods can be bought? How many tögrög will be left? | Mongolia, Математик III (Grade 3), printed p. 27 |

**Sub lesson map (added 2026-11-08, whole-arc rule):** the
rows above already stocked the size climb; the text now
teaches it as Sub lesson 4, carried by the Indonesia Grade 5
row (column subtraction to 100,000), the Ethiopia Grade 4
rows (subtracting to 1,000,000 with borrowing — worked
51,278 − 28,051 = 23,227; teacher-guide worked
678,803 − 569,740 = 109,063), the North Macedonia Grade 5
rows (to 1,000,000 without and with regrouping) and the
Rwanda Primary 6 row (beyond 1,000,000 with
borrowing/regrouping). No existing row changed.

**Acceptance count: taught in 67 countries (Egypt, Jordan, Libya, Kazakhstan, Greece, Armenia, Vietnam, Ghana, Mauritania, Laos, Yemen, Indonesia, Nicaragua, Ethiopia, South Sudan, Iraq, Bangladesh, Nepal, Cambodia, Kuwait, North Macedonia, Iran, China, Pakistan, Syria, Saudi Arabia, Rwanda, Sudan, India, Mongolia, Venezuela, Afghanistan, Uzbekistan, Ecuador, Albania, Bolivia, Tajikistan, Palestine, Turkey, Tanzania, South Africa, Sierra Leone, El Salvador, Cyprus, Colombia, Angola, Moldova, Australia, Azerbaijan, Bhutan, Cabo Verde, Costa Rica, Cuba, Bulgaria, Eritrea, Chile, Dominican Republic, Fiji, Honduras, Iceland, Peru, Serbia, Suriname, Papua New Guinea, Sweden, Timor-Leste, Turkmenistan).**. Egypt carries subtraction from its beginnings in Primary 1 (Japanese-collaboration series) — taking away, comparison, subtraction with zero, subtracting three numbers — through subtraction by using 10 in Term 2, and the column algorithm whole in Primary 2 — vertical subtraction and checking by addition in the new series, story problems and subtracting without regrouping in the old Discover book, borrowing from hundreds to tens, borrowing in both places, borrowing across zero — with 3- and 4-digit subtraction in Primary 3 (the Primary 1–3 rows were completed by the 2026-11-08 re-verification sweep). Jordan carries subtracting without regrouping in Grade 1 and with regrouping in Grade 2. Libya carries subtracting without regrouping to 100 in Grade 1, regrouping to 1000 in Grade 2, and to 10,000 in Grade 3. Kazakhstan carries the written column algorithm on named cases in Grade 2. Greece carries vertical subtraction with borrowing in Grade 1 Teuxos B. Armenia carries subtracting with place transitions in Grade 2. Vietnam carries subtracting without regrouping in Grade 1 and with regrouping in Grade 2. Ghana carries subtracting to 20 as teacher evidence — and its workbook's printed '15 − 3 = 18' is flagged wrong in the filing and is never taught. Mauritania carries subtraction with and without carrying in Grade 2. Other read countries teach subtracting inside their own Grade 1–3 books and join by merge.** Laos carries subtraction from take-away stories within 10 and crossing 10 by splitting to 10 in Grade 1, through 2-digit borrowing and 3-digit place-value subtraction in Grade 2, to whole-number and decimal subtraction in Grade 5. Yemen carries subtraction from take-away within 10 in Grade 1 through borrowing within 1000, 10,000 and 999,999, with estimating and checking by addition, to hundreds of millions in Grade 5. Indonesia carries subtraction from taking away to 10 and to 20 in Grade 1, through subtraction under 20 in Grade 2, column subtraction to 100 with borrowing in Grade 3 and to 1,000 in Grade 4, to column subtraction to 100,000 in Grade 5, then integer, fraction and decimal subtraction in Grade 7 (Merdeka and K13 Semester 1). Nicaragua carries subtraction from taking away within 10 in Grade 1, through borrowing to 100 in Grade 2 and to 1,000 in Grade 3, to a 4–5-digit worked box in Grade 4 (its only subtraction teaching there), and signed-number subtraction in Grade 7. Ethiopia carries subtraction from subtracting to 10 with the addition–subtraction relationship in Grade 1, through subtracting to 1000 without borrowing and with borrowing in Grade 2, to subtracting to 10,000 in Grade 3 and to 1,000,000 with borrowing in Grade 4. South Sudan carries subtraction from taking away without and with regrouping in Grade 1 (90 − 10 = 80; 188 − 69 = 119), through number-line subtraction and subtraction without borrowing in Grade 2, to subtraction to 4 digits in Grade 3 (6981 − 2221), and subtraction of fractions in Secondary 1. Iraq carries subtraction from to 10 and to 20 in Grade 1 — no regrouping — through regrouping to 999 in Grade 2 and to 9999 in Grade 3, to subtraction within millions in Grade 4, and to subtracting large numbers within billions in Grade 5. Bangladesh carries subtraction from the concept within 10 including subtraction of zero in Class 1, through subtraction with borrowing to 3 digits in Class 2 and borrowing across zeros in Class 3, to 5-digit subtraction in Class 4, and subtraction of integers in Class 6. Nepal carries subtraction from take-away to 5, 9, 10 and 19 and two-digit subtraction without borrowing in Grade 1, through subtraction with borrowing in Grade 2 (both books, including borrowing across zero in the Nepali book) and to 4 digits in Grade 3, to 5–6 digit subtraction with borrowing checked by adding back in Grade 4, and subtraction of fractions and decimals in Grades 5–6. Cambodia carries subtraction whole from single digits in Grade 1 through decimals in Grades 4–6, with subtracting negatives as the motive for negative numbers in Grade 7. Kuwait carries subtraction up the same ladder as addition — from 10 in Grade 1, to 100 across Grades 1–2, 3-digit numbers in Grade 2, to 10,000 in Grade 3, to millions in Grade 4, whole numbers and decimals in Grade 5, integers in Grade 6 and rational numbers in Grade 8. North Macedonia carries subtraction at Grade 1 (2022) — differences to 5, to 10 and to 20, built on the link between addition and subtraction from the start. Iran carries subtraction from the first written expressions in Grade 1 (§14) through borrowing in Grade 2 to larger numbers in Grade 3, and keeps it in use through the primary years.

China carries subtraction from Grade 1 borrowing work and the subtraction table to the Grade 4 part-relations and the chained-subtraction simplification.

Pakistan (Punjab) carries subtraction from the Primer's “take one away” to 9-digit column subtraction in Class 5 and integer subtraction in Class 6.

Syria builds subtraction as the missing part before the − sign, then as fact families in Grade 1 Semester 2, borrowing in two named steps in Grade 2, columns to four digits in Grade 3 (including borrowing across zeros) and five in Grade 4, and subtraction of rationals as adding the opposite in Grade 7. Three printed slips are taught corrected where they occur: the Grade 1 guitar premise (a guitar has 6 strings, not 5), the Grade 2 Semester 1 drill table's impossible 83 − 92, and the Grade 2 Semester 2 review's transposed check-additions.

Saudi Arabia teaches subtraction as the mirror of addition from Grade 1 — part–part–whole models, counting back and the number line — ties it to addition through fact families in Grade 2, regroups across zeros in Grade 3, and checks every column subtraction by adding back in Grade 4, with the older 1445 Term-3 volume witnessing the Grade 2 Part Two chapter.

Rwanda teaches subtraction as a staged climb — within 200, 500 and 999 in Year 2, to 2,000, 5,000 and 10,000 in Year 3 with one-step word problems, column subtraction in P4, column subtraction of 6/7-digit numbers in P5, subtraction beyond 1,000,000 in P6, and subtraction of natural numbers and of integers in Senior 1.

Sudan builds subtraction inside 9 in Grade 1 by crossing out pictures, with horizontal and vertical forms, then two-digit subtraction without borrowing, subtraction within 999 in Grade 2 and within 9999 in Grade 3.

India carries subtraction through its NCERT ladder — Math-Magic Class 1–4 and Mathematics Class 6 — including taking away; comparing by subtraction; subtraction with hundreds cards; differences of jumps in cm.


Mongolia enters through its ministry textbooks Математик I, II, III, V (Grades 1, 2, 3, 5): subtraction as taking away; subtracting in ones; difference by crossing out.

Venezuela enters through the Colección Bicentenario, the MPPE's one-book-per-grade course: *Contemos... 1, 2, 3 y 4* (Grade 1) and *Triángulos, rectángulos y algo más...* (Grade 2). From its books: from Grade 1: subtraction as taking away — taking seeds/plants from a collection: 10 − 3 and follow-up takings; 8 − 2 = 6 with minuendo, sustraendo, diferencia named; from Grade 2: subtraction as taking away and as difference — difference problems: 100 − 96 = 4 worked three ways (column subtraction, complement, check by addition).

Afghanistan enters through its Ministry of Education course — the Grade 1 course in two language editions (Pashto and Dari, one work with identical page maps) and Dari student books for Grades 4, 6, 7, 8, 10, 11 and 12, all printing year 1398 HS except the Grade 8 book, which prints 1399 HS on its cover and title page and 1398 HS on its specifications page. From its books: from Grade 1: subtraction as taking away: crossing out pictures (Lesson 15); subtraction with the - and = signs; subtraction in horizontal and vertical forms (Lesson 16); mixed addition and subtraction drill (worked 5-3=2); mixed addition and subtraction with missing numbers; subtracting zero: n - 0 = n (Lesson 18); circle the correct difference (worked examples); mixed addition and subtraction drill to 9; addition and subtraction from pictures (Lesson 19); subtracting round tens: 30 - 10 = 20, 40 - 20 = 20, 50 - 20 = 30 (Lesson 41); subtracting round tens: practice (70 - 30 = 40 worked); subtracting a two-digit number from a two-digit number, no borrowing (Lesson 42); two-digit - two-digit practice, horizontal and vertical; mixed two-digit addition and subtraction: column practice; mixed two-digit addition and subtraction: horizontal practice; word problems: subtraction stories and the numbers between (Lesson 43, problems 7, 10-12); from Grade 4: review: subtraction in vertical and horizontal form (Lesson 4); subtraction of numbers to a milliard (Lesson 11); checking a subtraction (Lesson 12). The Grade 1 Dari edition's own differing exercise items are stocked as Dari-edition rows above.

Albania enters through its Mediaprint teacher's books (Libër Mësuesi, in Albanian) — TEACHER EVIDENCE only: no pupil's book is in hand for any grade, so Albania is never cited as a pupil-textbook source; what the teacher's books evidence is what is taught and in what order, through their lesson plans, worked answers and answer keys. From its books: from Grade 1 (teacher's book): Subtraction and difference: subtraction as inverse of addition (15 − 7 = ? thought as 7 + ? = 15); partition,…; Chapter opener, Diskuto: understand subtraction as counting back and taking away; find 2 more or 2 less than…; Subtraction: take-away with cubes and rhymes; 10 − 1 = 9, 9 − 2 = 7, 7 − 5 = 2, 8 − 3 = 5, 10 − 3 = 7; check…; Counting back: add/subtract a one-digit number counting one by one forwards and back; from 12 count back to 6…; Finding the difference: difference as "how many more are needed"; towers of 5 and 3 have difference 2; jumps…; Number triples 8, 5, 3 give 8 − 3 = 5 and 8 − 5 = 3; difference-triangle with 1–6 (5 − 2 = 3 etc.); bee…; Provov veten: subtraction as counting back, one-digit subtractions, difference as "how many more needed";…. from Grade 5 (teacher's book): Subtraction of numbers (column method). from Grade 6 (teacher's book): : adding and subtracting by completing (compensation) (plans 43–44).

Bolivia enters through the Área Matemática sections of its Ministerio de Educación (Estado Plurinacional de Bolivia) Textos de Aprendizaje — the student books for Grades 7, 8, 10 and 12, each the maths section of that year's integrated year book, keeping the whole book's printed foliation, with no imprint or ISBN printed in any of them — and through the Compendio para Maestras y Maestros 2023–2024, the ministry's teacher compilation of the official student texts for all six Secundaria years (Grades 7–12), cited as teacher-compilation evidence only, never as or in place of a pupil text; its Secundaria 3 and Secundaria 5 sections are the only in-hand source for Grades 9 and 11, whose student texts are not in the record. From its books: from Grade 7: Resta de números enteros.

Tajikistan enters through its Ministry-approved pupil's textbooks in Tajik (Cyrillic), all published in Dushanbe — Математика for Grade 5 (Маориф, 2022) and Grade 6 (5th edition, Маориф, 2021), Алгебра for Grades 7 (4th improved edition, 2023) and 8 (4th edition, 2023), and Геометрия — the continuation of stereometry — for Grade 11 (3rd edition, Лавҳ, 2020). From its books: from Grade 5: Subtraction defined as finding the unknown addend; Subtraction of multi-digit numbers in columns.

Palestine enters through the pupil's books of its Ministry of Education (Curriculum Centre, Ramallah), all in Arabic — Mathematics Grade 1 in two parts (both fourth edition, 2020), Grade 10 in two parts (Part One third edition, 2020; Part Two second edition, 2019 — the held parts are different editions and are cited as such), and the Grade 12 Technological-stream book (التكنولوجي, first edition, 2019 — the book's own name for its stream, never "Information Technology"). No ISBN is printed in any of the five books. From its books: from Grade 1 Part One: subtraction within 9 as taking away from a pictured set; horizontal subtraction sentences; represent subtractions with materials and find the result; complete subtraction sentences; difference comparison: how many more red cubes than the other colour; word problem: 9 roses, 3 given away, how many remain; addition and subtraction are inverse: from one addition sentence write two subtraction sentences and back; review: build addition from subtraction; code-table of subtraction results spelling a Palestinian city; difference word problems (8 vs 3 apricots); from Grade 1 Part Two: subtraction within 10 consolidated: take counters from 10, write the subtraction sentence; 10 − n facts; subtraction on the number line: 10 steps forward, 7 back; remainder word problems within 10 (10 flowers, 3 yellow; 10 eggs, 6 used); subtraction within 18 (I) by crossing out (17 apples, 7 used — how many remain); mental subtraction within 18 with comparison of differences; subtraction within 18 (II): two-step money problem (10 + 4 dinars, spend 8); subtract through ten: 14 − 8 = 10 − 8 + 4; split the teen number into 10 + ones and subtract from the 10; subtraction within 18 (III): check subtraction by addition (15 − 7 = 8 because 8 + 7 = 15); fact families: choose the three numbers from four that form a subtraction family; subtract and check by adding; missing-number word problem: 11 stars held, 15 needed for the prize; unit review: missing subtrahend/minuend items; think-puzzles (sum 11 and difference 1; a number minus 13 gives 3).

Barbados enters as CURRICULUM EVIDENCE only, through the *Primary Mathematics Syllabus* (Classes 1–4) of its Ministry of Education and Human Resource Development — its syllabi require subtraction of whole numbers at every class — up to 999 at Class 1 (with subtraction facts to 18 recalled and addition shown as the inverse), from four-digit numbers at Class 2, up to 99,999 at Class 3 and up to 9,999,999 at Class 4, with and without regrouping. A syllabus states what is to be taught, in what order and to what depth; it teaches nothing itself. Barbados publishes no government pupil textbooks — primary mathematics books are commercial titles parents buy, and the syllabi's Suggested Texts appendices name them (*Caribbean Primary Mathematics* Levels 1–6; *Nelson Primary Maths for Caribbean Schools* 1–4; *Steps To Common Entrance Mathematics* 1–3) as named by the syllabus only: none is in hand and none is cited. Barbados is therefore named in this lesson's countries line and its syllabus requirements are cited in the table as curriculum evidence, but Barbados is not counted in the taught-in figure.

Turkey enters through the pupil's textbooks of its Millî Eğitim Bakanlığı (MEB — Ministry of National Education), all in Turkish, first volumes (1. Kitap) only — Grade 3 (ISBN 978-975-11-9897-6, TTKB approval 13/07/2026), Grade 6 (ISBN 978-975-11-8773-4, TTKB approval 30.06.2025) and Grade 7 (ISBN 978-975-11-9899-0, TTKB approval 1.07.2026); no edition, printing or year line is printed in any of the three books, and the grades' second volumes (2. Kitap) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. From its books: from Grade 3: column subtraction without borrowing, terms named: 246 − 132 = 114 with minuend, subtrahend, difference labelled and modelled in blocks; which term is the biggest?; subtraction needing borrowing set up: 593 seats, 346 tickets sold — how many seats are unsold; start from the ones; what to do when the ones digit is too small; borrowing (onluk bozma) step by step in base-ten blocks: 327 − 141 = 186 — take a hundred as 10 tens (12 tens − 4 tens), then 2 hundreds − 1 hundred; when tens cannot be subtracted, take from the previous place; mental subtraction game: two half-circles of numbers (big numbers 500–1000, small numbers 99–501) — pick one from each and subtract mentally, a point for a correct answer; mental subtraction by splitting into place values: 255 − 123 = (200−100) + (50−20) + (5−3) = 132; and by counting back in tens: 255 − 60 by six backward 10-steps (255, 245, … 195); analyse additions and differences of food masses in the same table frame: cucumber + egg, egg + bread + walnut, cucumber − tomato, tomato − bread; can two-digit and three-digit addition be done the same way?; find a missing addend: 178 + 251 = 429 lemons — if one picker’s count were unknown, which operation finds it; a missing addend is found by subtraction; find the first addend of 243 + 336 = 579 by modelling the subtraction it reverses; addition and subtraction undo each other, stated in full: 322 + 100 = 422, so 422 − 100 = 322 and 422 − 322 = 100; taking an addend away from the sum gives the other addend; the minuend equals difference + subtrahend; missing-number operations both ways (… + 450 = 858; 192 + … = 300; … − 218 = 583; 713 − … = 408); rewrite 147 + 321 with each term moved into the subtraction slots; carry a sum's terms into a subtraction and a difference's terms into an addition (408 + 450; 583 − 218); from Grade 7: Subtraction as difference of positions on a vertical number line (bird above sea level, fish below); Matching real situations to addition vs subtraction forms with signed amounts (bank balance −100 TL); Integer subtraction rule: subtracting is adding the opposite sign of the number subtracted.

Tanzania enters through the pupil's book of its Tanzania Institute of Education (TIE) — *Mathematics Standard Five — Pupil's Book*, the English-medium Standard Five book (© TIE 2019, published 2019, reprinted 2021; ISBN 978-9987-09-109-6; Certificate of Approval No. 282, approved by the Ministry of Education, Science and Technology on 5 December 2019, prepared to the 2016 primary Mathematics Syllabus). It is the only Tanzania mathematics book in hand: the other 20 recorded works — among them the Swahili-medium Hisabati Std 5, a separate book from this English one — are waiting-for-file and are cited nowhere. From its book: from Standard Five: subtraction as the opposite of addition; subtraction without regrouping, horizontal and vertical; subtraction on the counting basin and by expanded form; subtraction with regrouping; word problems of deficits and differences.

Armenia's stock in this lesson is widened by the full re-verification sweep of 2026-11-08, which re-read Armenia's maths books page by page and rebuilt the placements from the PDFs. Newly stocked from its Mathematics 1, Part 1 (student textbook-notebook, 2025); Mathematics 2, Part I (student); Mathematics 5 (student): subtraction (first introduction, on objects); subtraction (continued); subtracting two-digit numbers; subtraction of natural numbers; moving left on the coordinate ray; when the subtrahend is a sum.

## Morocco added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Morocco's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 books, all of them teacher guides or teacher editions (Dar Attakafa, Casablanca); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 610 teaching items. No Moroccan pupil maths textbook is in hand: the pupil's books are commercial print, and the library's systematic free shelf is Dar Attakafa's teacher-guide library. Every Morocco item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states the pupil book's lesson, its content and its order — cited as the evidence kind it is; Morocco is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Moroccan pupil books are in hand and read. Set reconciliation, as the placements prove it: the 32 are distinct works (Le Monde des Maths ×6, L'Explicite en Maths ×6, Al Moufid primary ×3, Al Marjie primary ×2, Al Moufid collège ×3, Switch On! guides ×6, Switch On! Every Day booklets ×6; French 27 books, Arabic 5); textbooks.tsv carries only 28 of the 32 — a library-record gap, reported here and not edited (merges never edit the records). The primary guides all teach the one official revised programme (July 2020) — 32 lessons per grade, Grades 1–6, stated independently by up to four series per grade; the collège guides give the BIOF programme distributions for Grades 7–9. Read depth, as the placements declare it: the Arabic guides' legacy font encoding makes pdftotext return mojibake, so their contents were read from rendered pages; Books 6, 7 and 25 place their own year's programme distribution only (their readings of the other collège years are excluded, so nothing is double-counted); Book 31 places the distinct rows of its yearly programme table, a coarser grain than the lesson-by-lesson guides. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct lesson/topic the guide teaches the teacher to deliver, and its named exclusions — method/framework chapters, evaluation/bilan/support apparatus and the Switch On confirmation tests, Switch On 1's graphisme weeks, bibliographies, indexes and recording tables — are stocked nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 19 teacher-evidence items from 11 of the guides (Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 8, Book 10, Book 11, Book 27, Book 28, Book 31; Grades 1, 2, 3).

**Book 1:** *Le Monde des Maths 1, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 1re année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 1, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 1,775,505 bytes, 201 PDF pages. The collection follows the revised curriculum/programmes of July 2020 (32 lessons in 6 units over 4 domains); items are the guide's 32 numbered lessons, printed pp. from its Table des matières. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 2:** *Le Monde des Maths 2, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 2e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 2, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 9,555,550 bytes. Same collection and July-2020 revised programme as Book 1; items are its 32 numbered lessons — printed pp. from its Table des matières where the sommaire prints them, later lessons cited by sequence (seq.). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 3:** *L'Explicite en Maths CP, Guide pédagogique* (CP = Grade 1) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 21,092,403 bytes, 116 PDF pages. The explicit-teaching method guide for the pioneer-schools programme; its Chapter 3 gives worked lesson sheets for the SAME 32-lesson Grade 1 programme as Book 1 — a shared programme, two different guides, cited separately, never merged. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 4:** *المفيد في الرياضيات (Al Moufid fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذة والأستاذ* (teacher guide, Grade 1, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 7,559,907 bytes; new edition September 2019, with ministry approval panel. Its contents table lists the same 32-lesson revised programme as Books 1 and 3, in Arabic; the PDF's legacy font encoding makes pdftotext return mojibake, so the contents were read from rendered pages. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 5:** *L'Explicite en Maths CE1, Guide pédagogique* (CE1 = Grade 2) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 18,580,797 bytes. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 2 programme corresponding to Book 2. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 8:** *Switch On! 1re année, Guide de l'enseignant* (teacher guide, Grade 1, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 3,979,650 bytes, 88 PDF pages. A 5-minute daily calculation-drill guide (Japanese method, dir. Prof. Akira Sato); items are the distinct drill strands in its Sommaire (tracks A and B, weeks 1–34). Its graphisme weeks 1–2 (pencil-stroke practice, no mathematics content) and its confirmation tests (assessment apparatus) are excluded in the placements. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *المرجع في الرياضيات (Al Marjie fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذة والأستاذ* (teacher guide, Grade 3, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 18,309,585 bytes, 351 PDF pages. Its contents (read from rendered pages — legacy font encoding) list 32 numbered lessons in 6 units, the Grade 3 programme. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 11:** *Switch On! 2e année, Guide de l'enseignant* (teacher guide, Grade 2, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 8,214,244 bytes, 88 PDF pages. Items are the distinct drill strands in its Sommaire (weeks 1–34); the confirmation tests are excluded in the placements as assessment apparatus. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 27:** *Le Monde des Maths 3, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 3e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 3, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 6,204,899 bytes. Items are its 32 numbered lessons — printed pp. from its Table des matières where printed, later units by sequence; the Grade 3 programme, corresponding to Book 10. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 28:** *L'Explicite en Maths CE2, Guide pédagogique* (CE2 = Grade 3) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 8,578,274 bytes. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 3 programme, corresponding to Books 10 and 27. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 31:** *المرجع في الرياضيات (Al Marjie fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذ(ة)* (teacher guide, Grade 2, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 55,959,139 bytes, 393 PDF pages. Items are the distinct rows of its yearly programme table (guide printed pp. 48–50, read from rendered pages), which states the Grade 2 content by domain with learning objectives — a coarser grain than the lesson-by-lesson guides, as the placements declare. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

## El Salvador added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read El Salvador's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — the MINEDUCYT **ESMATE** programme (Proyecto de Mejoramiento de los Aprendizajes en Matemática en Educación Básica y Educación Media, MINEDUCYT–JICA, 2015–2019): **63 filed entries = 43 distinct works** — 16 student files covering the 11 grade textbooks (Grades 1–11; Grade 1 in 4 part-files, Grade 2 in 3), 25 teacher guides, and 2 JICA materials documents (the materials-use guideline and the Japan–El Salvador unit list) filed 11 times each, byte-identical, MD5-proved — the 2 documents are programme apparatus with no teaching content, cited once each in the country record and stocked in no lesson. The read placed 383 teaching items: 281 from the student textbooks and 102 teacher-evidence items from the guides (one per unit per grade). In the sections below the student books are cited as Books S1–S13 and the guides as Books T1–T12, in grade order. El Salvador COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-textbook rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guides reproduce the student lessons with teaching plans, worked solutions and a unit test per unit — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count. Book errors: none that changes a placement (the Grade 9 book's history opener dates al-Khwārizmī to c. 825 BC; he lived c. 780–850 AD — a front-matter error in an excluded motivation essay, in no teaching item, so nothing is taught from it). The placements' named exclusions — *Recortables* cut-out pages, covers, credits and unit-opener history/motivation essays, the GeoGebra practice lessons of Grades 10–11, end-of-unit and end-of-trimester tests in the student books, teacher-guide front matter, and the Grade 6 book's *Repaso* review sections — are stocked nowhere.

Into this lesson the placements stock 14 items (13 from student books, 1 teacher evidence) from Book S1, Book S2, Book S3, Book T3, Book S5.

## Cyprus added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cyprus's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **75 distinct works: 22 pupil textbooks (primary Grades 1–6, part-books) and 53 teacher guides (Οδηγός Εκπαιδευτικού), all Cyprus Ministry of Education (Υπουργείο Παιδείας), in Greek** — and placed 281 teaching items: 228 from the pupil books and 53 teacher-evidence items (one per guide, worded from the guide's stated success indicators, ΔΕΙΚΤΕΣ ΕΠΙΤΥΧΙΑΣ). SET CORRECTION: the expansion map recorded Cyprus Maths as 22 student + 43 teacher = 65 in hand; the records' union proves 22 student + 53 teacher = **75** (staging books.json carries 64 files = 22 student + 42 teacher, all secured in GitHub; textbooks.tsv adds 11 further teacher guides held as git-blob files that are in no books.json: Grade 1 Units 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, Grade 2 Unit 1, Grade 4 Unit 1) — the correction is recorded here; the library records themselves are not edited (merges never edit the records). In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S22 and the guides as Books T1–T53, in grade order. Cyprus COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states the pupil book's unit, its indicators and its lesson organisation — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). The read's three flagged claims are carried exactly: (1) the map's “65” is corrected above; (2) the Grade 3 Unit 1 teacher guide's indicator Αρ2.1 states quantities “to 10,000” where its pupil book's Unit 1 teaches to 1,000 — the guide's figure is NOT confirmed by the pupil book and is worded as the guide's own claim in that guide's row, never as pupil teaching; (3) three guides carry the books' own unit-number mislabels — the Grade 4 Unit 3 guide's contents header reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 3 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 9 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 8” — the contents match their cover units, so the rows record the true units with the errors noted in the book entries, taught corrected, never as printed. Production date stamps inside three files (Grade 5 Part 1 “22/06/2026”, Grade 6 Part 1's InDesign stamp 05/03/2026, Grade 6 Part 3 “01/07/2026”) are PDF production stamps in the ministry files as held, not edition dates. The placements' named exclusions — front matter, icon keys, contents pages, cut-out card appendices (“Για τη σελίδα …”), Διαμορφωτική Αξιολόγηση (formative assessment) pages and enrichment sets repeating unit content — are stocked nowhere; the apparent “330C/20C” in the Grade 3 Part 1 enrichment is the degree sign merging with the digits in the text layer (33 °C falling by 2 °C), not a book error. Teacher-guide coverage in hand: Grades 1–3 Units 1–12 complete, Grade 4 Units 1–3 only, Grade 5 Units 1–4, Grade 6 Units 1–10; the further guides that exist only as ministry hub links (Grade 4 Units 4+, Grade 5 Units 5+, Grade 6 Units 11+) are not in hand, not read and not counted. Cyprus's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (75/75) has been read and placed; if the hub-link guides are later secured, Cyprus reverts to PARTIAL until they are read.

## Sri Lanka added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sri Lanka's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs in two passes — **94 files / 66 distinct works: 26 pupil textbooks and 40 teacher guides, all Sri Lanka Educational Publications Department (EPD) / National Institute of Education (NIE), in the country's three media — English, Sinhala and Tamil** — and placed 479 items: 289 from the pupil books and 190 teacher-evidence items (the secondary guides at one item per term of the guide's own lesson sequence; the primary guides at one item per syllabus competency group or per part). Pass 1 read the expansion map's 59 files (32 works); Pass 2 pinned 35 further in-hand files the map's subject filter had missed — 34 NIE teacher guides for Grades 1–13 and one pupil textbook (Book S26, filed under the subject "Science for Technology") — and read every one. In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S26 and the guides as Books T1–T40. Sri Lanka COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states its syllabus's competencies, its lesson sequence and its teaching instructions — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Set facts carried exactly: (1) the Grade 9 Sinhala pupil book is held only as chapter files of two different printings (Books S9–S10) whose shared chapter 20 is a different chapter in each, and NO chapter-26 file exists in either split — the English twin's chapter 26 is Algebraic fractions; the hole is carried as a set gap, not as read; (2) the 2018 Sinhala Grade 9 teacher guide is filed twice (two scans of one work) and is placed once (Book T2), and the file held as the Grade 7 2008 manual's "Tamil" copy is byte-identical to the English manual (same git blob SHA) and is cited once, as the English manual (Book T10); (3) the year-less Tamil Grade 4 and Grade 5 guides are proved by byte size to be NIE's 2015 editions, not the far larger 2019/2020 scans NIE also lists (Books T39–T40); (4) the 1976 Grade 12 Mathematics Part I scan (Book S23) ends with some six pages of an unrelated German textbook's verb tables (PDF pp. 271–276) — its mathematics runs to PDF p. 270 and its last chapters were sampled, not contents-verified; (5) the Grade 1 Sinhala 2026 guide is in hand as a Term 1 volume only (Book T33) — its Terms 2–3 are stocked nowhere — and the Grade 1 Tamil 2016 guide (Book T31) is marked DRAFT on its cover and is placed as filed; (6) the Grade 11 guide's Term 2 table labels one lesson (competency 23.6, inside the geometry cluster) "Central limit theorem" — recorded as printed in Book T19's entry; no row in this course rests on that label; (7) Grade 10 Mathematics Part I (English) and Grade 7 Mathematics Part I (Tamil, 2007–2015 edition) are NOT in hand in any filing — library gaps, not unread books; their twins in the other medium are held and read (Books S17 and S13/S14). The placements' named exclusions — revision exercises, answers, glossaries, assessment apparatus, and the guides' specimen lesson plans and school-based assessment instruments beyond their sequences — are stocked nowhere. Sri Lanka's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (94/94 files) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (0 from pupil books, 9 teacher evidence) from Book T31, Book T32, Book T34, Book T35, Book T36, Book T37, Book T38, Book T39, Book T40.

The filed copy is marked DRAFT on its cover; it is placed as filed, and the mark is recorded here.

## Colombia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Colombia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **27 distinct works: 26 student books and 1 teacher guide, all Colombia Ministerio de Educación Nacional (MEN), in Spanish** — Escuela Nueva Matemáticas cartillas Grades 1–5 (11), Nivelemos Matemáticas student workbooks Grades 1–4 (4), Aulas sin fronteras Grade 6 Unidad 1 student book and its teacher guide (2), Postprimaria student guides Grades 6–9 (4), Secundaria Activa student modules Grades 6–9 (4), and Vamos a Aprender Matemáticas 10 and 11 (2) — 3,928 PDF pages — and placed 293 teaching items: 288 from the student books and 5 teacher-evidence items from the one teacher guide (Book 23). SET CORRECTIONS carried exactly: (1) the expansion map's “28 student + 1 teacher = 29” over-counts by 2 — textbooks.tsv carries duplicate records of Escuela Nueva Grado 1 Cartilla 1 and Grado 2 Cartilla 1 (old blob/main rows for the same two works that also landed as release assets); the distinct set is 27 works and the duplicates are cited once (the library records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records); (2) the set in hand contains NO Grade 3 Cartilla 3 — Grade 3 holds Cartillas 1–2 plus the Nivelemos Grade 3 workbook, and Cartilla 2 completes Unidades 6–9; nothing in hand is a mislabelled Cartilla 3, and no never-published ruling is needed or made; (3) Nivelemos Matemáticas Grado 1 (Book 10) has NO asset in any GitHub release — it was read from the MEN source file itself, landed at exactly the census size (5,020,415 bytes) with EOF intact; the library side still owes that file a GitHub landing. The two Vamos a Aprender books (Books 26–27) are image-only viewer assemblies with no text layer, read from rendered pages (covers, printed contents pp. 6–7 and body spot-checks — Matemáticas 10 printed p. 110 “Teorema del seno”, Matemáticas 11 printed p. 120 “Derivada de una función en un punto”); printed page = PDF page in the sampled range. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–27 in the placements' order. Colombia COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-book rows; Book 23's rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guide companions Book 15 with teaching plans, activity scripts and answers and teaches no student content itself — cited as the evidence kind it is and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Book errors: none material at placement grain — the Secundaria Activa Grade 9 module's contents page misprints two of its own headings (“Captítulo”, “Undiad 2”); the content is unaffected and rows use the corrected unit names. The placements' named exclusions — presentation letters, “how this book works” pages, author/imprint pages, transversal civics/health/environment themes, practice/evaluation apparatus (“Practica más”, “Evaluación del aprendizaje”, answer keys) and the Escuela Nueva “Estándares/Competencias” boxes at each unidad opening (MEN's own curriculum-standard statements; the taught content is placed at the guías themselves) — are stocked nowhere. The Círculos de Aprendizaje integrated books and the Retos para Gigantes Transición weekly guides are NOT in this set (filed as integrado, not matematicas) and are stocked nowhere. Colombia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (27/27) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 5, Book 10, Book 15.

## Andorra added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Andorra's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them official Escola Andorrana curriculum programmes published by decree in the Butlletí Oficial del Principat d'Andorra (BOPA); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 679 curriculum-requirement items. A programme states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Andorra item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a curriculum requirement, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is; Andorra is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Andorran pupil books are in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded 3 books in hand; the true set is 4 — the grades 1–6 programme (Decret 376/2021, Annex VII) is held in the GitHub repo but sits in no staging books.json, which is why the map missed it. All four programmes are Andorra's OWN (Govern d'Andorra, in Catalan); the Spanish and French systems also taught in Andorra (Spain's Anaya and Innovamat manuals; France's Le Livre Scolaire) are list-only names on official school book lists — no file is held, so they are cited nowhere in this course and credited to no country — and the Escola Andorrana itself has no commercial pupil-textbook series, so programmes are all that exist to hold. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct stated requirement — each specific competency, and each distinct entry in the programmes' learning-resource tables, tagged by cycle where the table splits by cycle — and a procedure entry that merely restates a concept entry is folded into it, not double-counted. The placements' named exclusions — the decree preambles, the introductions and competency rationale, the didactic orientations, the assessment criteria and end-of-cycle expectations, the synthesis tables (verified verbatim repeats of the competency sections), the attitudes/values lists, Book 1's glossary and opening summary table, and the Council of Europe cross-references — are stocked nowhere. Book 3's synthesis table words its C2 end-of-cycle expectation as modelling "situacions cientificotècniques" (scientific-technical) inside the social-sciences programme — a copy-over slip in the decree as published; Book 3's competency section itself is consistently social-sciences, and the slip is copied into no row below. Book 2 states its cone/polyhedron sections twice (forms table and models table, printed pp. 32–33) — counted once in the placements and stocked once. Printed page = PDF page in all four books (Books 2–4 carry the BOPA folio; Book 1's Portal PDF is paginated at the foot the same way). Into this lesson the placements stock 6 teacher-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Programa de matemàtiques de primera ensenyança* (curriculum programme, Grades 1–6) — Andorra, Govern d'Andorra, Escola Andorrana; Decret 376/2021, Annex VII; ISBN 978-99920-0-934-5; 688,455 bytes, 49 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

## Angola added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Angola's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books, all pupil textbooks: Matemática, Manuais da 1.ª–6.ª Classe (Ministério da Educação — MED, Angola; updated editions of 2021, in Portuguese). There is no teacher guide in the Angola Maths set in hand, so every Angola row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Angola counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 229 teaching items. The expansion map's “6 student + 0 teacher = 6” is correct: download-lists/angola.md, the phase3-staging checklist, textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files all give the same six distinct pupil textbooks, and nothing else. None of the six PDFs was on local disk (pusher-secured only), so all six were fetched fresh for the read from the books' recorded second official source, the ministry's Xilonga host, byte-exact against the records. One set nuance: the portal's 32-manual set contains no 2.ª Classe Matemática at all — the 2.ª book in hand (Book 2) exists only as an unlinked Xilonga file (MAT02-LR.pdf) found by pattern probe on 2026-10-23. A Guia Prático Matemática 6ª exists only as an exact-link reader copy and is NOT in hand — cited nowhere; if one is ever secured it enters as teacher evidence only. The Book 2 and Book 4 PDFs carry a massively duplicated text layer (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the files; the content is fully legible and both books were read in full from it. Twelve book errors are recorded in the placements; they are carried in the book entries below and taught corrected. The placements' named exclusions — the apresentação (ministerial foreword letter) and anthem/flag/civic front matter, the bibliografia pages, pure colouring, tracing and copying practice with no new concept, the editorial production notes printed inside the books, and exercise sets as such (exercises practise the item under which they sit) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases: Books 1 and 3 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Books 2, 4, 5 and 6 — printed page = PDF page. Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 5.

**Book 1:** *Matemática — Manual da 1.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Editora Moderna, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-267-0; 4,293,873 bytes, 81 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

**Book 2:** *Matemática — Manual da 2.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-297-8; 3,437,647 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 23 calls the minuend “diminuendo ou aditivo” — “aditivo” is wrong for subtraction; the course teaches minuend. Editorial production notes are printed as lesson content on printed p. 50 — production matter, cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 3:** *Matemática — Manual da 3.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Progresso Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-270-0; 1,798,728 bytes, 85 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 21 repeats the minuend misnomer “diminuendo ou aditivo” (teach: minuend); printed p. 36's decimal-multiplication worked example is internally inconsistent — it states 4,15 × 2,3, lays out 3,15 and prints 7,245 (correct: 4,15 × 2,3 = 9,545); the exercise under “Noção de circunferência” at printed p. 44 repeats the parallel-lines exercise verbatim (a copy-paste error in the book); printed p. 62 prints the false line “20 m = 200 cm” (correct: 20 m = 2 000 cm); printed p. 64's formula under decâmetro repeats “1 hm = 100 m” (correct: 1 dam = 10 m).

**Book 4:** *Matemática — Manual da 4.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 4) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-296-1; 1,983,954 bytes, 112 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 43 claims division “is only possible if the dividend is a multiple of the divisor” (exact division only) — contradicted by Book 3's own division-with-remainder teaching; the course teaches division with remainder. Editorial production notes are printed as page content on printed pp. 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 28, 30 and 45 — production matter, cited nowhere; the Tema 3 heading is misprinted inside the book (at printed p. 102); PDF pp. 106–112 are blank/back matter apart from the bibliografia (printed p. 107), cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 5) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-84-8; 4,730,235 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 29's worked example 12 + 37 + 7 + 3 is printed as = 55 with a garbled regrouping (correct total: 59); printed p. 73's equivalence line prints 1/3 = 2/6 = 2/9 (the last term should be 3/9); printed p. 127's median worked example uses an ordered list containing 12, which is not in the exercise's data, and drops one 10 (the median is taught from the data as printed in the exercise: ordered 6, 7, 9, 10, 10, 10, 11, 13, 14 → median 10).

## Moldova added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Moldova's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **12 books, all pupil textbooks: the Matematică / Mathematics student manual for each of Grades 1–12 (Republic of Moldova, in Romanian). There is no teacher guide and no syllabus in the Moldova Maths set in hand, so every Moldova row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Moldova counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 394 teaching items. The expansion map's “12 student + 0 teacher” is correct: textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files reconcile exactly at 12 distinct works — Grades 3–12 (10 books) recorded in books.json, Grades 1–2 as git-blob files recorded in textbooks.tsv. The old closed-52 inventory's “Moldova link-only, 0 in hand” is refuted: all 12 books sit secured in the tensae-code/liqlearns-textbooks GitHub repo and were read from there for this expansion (Grades 3, 4 and 6–12 via the mid-size-books-8 release; Grades 1, 2 and 5 via the Contents API on their textbooks.tsv git-blob paths), byte-verified — MD5s match books.json exactly for the ten Grades 3–12 books. The Grades 10–12 books are dual-track (profil real / profil umanistic) throughout; each section is stocked once, as one Moldova row, not once per track. Book errors: none found that changes a placement; the Grade 9 file's PDF p. 178 carries a stray page from another book (a biology text page) bound after the back cover in the publisher's print file — a file artifact, not part of the mathematics manual, cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the “Cine seamănă culege” review blocks, the Recapitulare / sistematizare / evaluare sections, the “Să recapitulăm” summaries, the recap exercise sets and “Test sumativ” pages closing chapters, and the “Răspunsuri (și indicații)” answer keys — are stocked nowhere, except where a review section is a topic's only statement, which is then marked as such. Printed-page bases: Book 1 — printed page = PDF page; Book 2 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Book 3 — printed page = PDF page; Book 4 — printed page = PDF page; Book 5 — printed page = PDF page − 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 21 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 5.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Moldova, Ludmila Ursu, Ilie Lupu, Iulia Iasinschi, Editura Prut Internațional, 2014 (re-edition approved by Ministerial Order nr. 674 din 9 august 2010), ISBN 978-9975-54-150-3; 28,813,891 bytes, 136 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page).

**Book 2:** *Mathematics* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Moldova, Ministerul Educației al Republicii Moldova, Editura Prut Internațional, re-edition approved by Order nr. 544 din 8 iunie 2015; 24,017,939 bytes, 138 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

## Bahamas added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Bahamas' whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them Bahamas Ministry of Education curriculum or examination-syllabus documents; 0 pupil (student) textbooks and 0 teacher guides** — and placed 496 requirement items. A curriculum states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Bahamas item in this section is therefore CURRICULUM EVIDENCE — a statement of what the Bahamas requires, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is (Book 3's items are examination-SYLLABUS evidence, on the same standing); the Bahamas is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana/Barbados evidence-only precedent, until a Bahamas pupil book is in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded the in-hand set as “4 student + 0 teacher” — the count of 4 is right, the kind is wrong; all four documents are ministry requirement documents (kind = curriculum in both the staging books.json files and textbooks.tsv). The file the records title *BJC Mathematics Curriculum, Grades 7–9* is actually the *Bahamas Junior Certificate 044 Mathematics Syllabus 2003* — a 10-page scanned examination syllabus with no text layer, read visually page by page — and the held Senior High file is the July 2019 revision of the Grades 10–12 guidelines; both are placed as what they are. No pupil textbook or teacher guide is in hand: the pupil books Bahamas schools use are commercial (Hodder *Bahamas Primary Mathematics* Books 1–6 — existence verified in the records, no file held; Books 1–2 name Silver Burdett Ginn Mathematics and Harcourt Math in classroom use, and Book 4's bibliography names STP Caribbean Mathematics, Mathematics: A Complete Course, Fundamental Mathematics for the Caribbean and Nelson Caribbean Mathematics — named only, none held), and the one school-compiled pupil text with an exact link in the records (Akhepran International Academy, *Mathematics BJC Textbook — Grade 7*, 2023) was never downloaded and sits in no record set, so it is not part of this read and is cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered objective in each guidelines book's Scope of Work body — the Content column read with every objective — Book 3 at one item per numbered topic row of its Topics table and Book 4 at one item per CONCEPTS/TOPICS block; an objective restated in full on a continuation page is counted once, at its first full statement, and the two requirements the documents restate inside a second strand (Book 1 Grade 2 estimation, item 69; Book 2 Grade 5 rounding of decimals, item 256) are stocked as the placements place them, each row naming the restatement. The placements' named exclusions — Benchmarks, Scope-and-Sequence charts, pacing guides and Essential Questions in Books 1–2; the Problems of the Day sets, Materials Lists, assessment rubrics, the Children's Literature list and References (record items R1–R7 and R9–R11); Book 3's examination administration, its 19 general aims and its erratum sheet (R8); Book 4's Scope and Sequence charts, Glossary and Technology Integration pages — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 12 requirement-evidence items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Primary School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 1–3* (curriculum guidelines) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Department of Education, September 2010; 5,558,181 bytes, 263 PDF pages; Scope of Work printed page = PDF page − 51. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

**Book 2:** *Primary School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 4–6* (curriculum guidelines) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Department of Education, September 2010; 5,278,639 bytes, 328 PDF pages; Scope of Work printed page = PDF page − 54. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

The Bahamas' class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): all 4 in-hand documents are read and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand; it advances only when a pupil book file — a Hodder *Bahamas Primary Mathematics* volume, or the Akhepran Grade 7 BJC textbook whose exact link already sits in the download list — lands in hand and is read.

## Belize added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Belize's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 documents, all of them Belize Ministry of Education *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide* books (August 2012) — Infant 1, Standard 1 and Standard IV; 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 108 teaching items. Every document in the set is a teacher's guide: it states the learning outcomes pupils must reach and teaches the teacher how to teach each one (comments, sample lesson objectives, specific content, sample activities and assessment). Every Belize item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — Belize's guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; no Belize pupil textbook is in hand — cited as the evidence kind it is; Belize is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Belize pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the pupil books on the official National Textbook Program list (*My BZ Math* Infant 1–Standard 4, Macmillan; *Caribbean Primary Math* Bk 6 for Standard 5, Hodder; *Active Math* for Standard 6, Macmillan) are all recorded missing in the library files, and the guides' own textbook linkages point to the legacy pupil series *Primary Mathematics for Belize* (Hodder, by term codes Y1T1…/S1T1…), which is named by the guides only — not in hand, not read, and not a course source. Three further Maths documents — the *Primary curriculum: Mathematics* division webs (Lower Division, Middle Division, Upper Division) — are recorded at exact link only, never downloaded and in no books.json; they are not part of this read and are cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered learning outcome in each guide's Standards and Learning Outcomes section (35 outcomes in each guide, 105 in all) plus 3 items taught in a detailed section but stated in no outcome of that guide (items 64, 65 and 87, marked D); outcome numbering is continuous across the series (Infant 1 carries M1.1–M20.1, Standard 1 continues at M1.10, Standard IV at M1.16), which is why the codes do not restart at 1 in the later guides. Printed page = PDF page in all three guides. The placements' named exclusions — the guides' front matter (programme philosophy: goals, rationale, assessment discussion), strand introductions and Big Ideas prose, sample lesson objectives, sample assessments, sample activities and print/technology resource columns as apparatus, the shared manipulatives list, the websites lists, the shared Mathematics Glossary (carried in the placements as record item R2 — a teacher reference list, cited nowhere as content), and the standards each guide marks N/A at its level (Book 1: M2 place value and M13 equations; Book 2: M14 measurement concepts; Book 3: M5 position/ranking numbers and M14) — are stocked nowhere. The 14 book errors and inconsistencies found in the read (placements E1–E14) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: item 35 is placed as data interpretation, not probability, though the guide files it under its probability standard (E5); item 51's set language is taught with “complement” for the book's “compliment” (E6); item 66's many-to-one correspondence is taught as one symbol or unit standing for many objects, not the guide's function-style definition (E8); item 82 is stocked WITHOUT the outcome's fourth bullet (“expand powers with same base to add or subtract”), which is not a valid add/subtract procedure (E9); item 81 is placed as stated and flagged as vague and advanced for its level (E10); item 104 is placed as measurement and taught with the standard measurement meanings, the guide's own definition of precision being a data-management sense (E13); and item 106 follows the guide's own correct definitions — its assessment text's “medium” for median is an error (E12). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 3.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Infant 1* (teacher guide, Grade 1) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 738,164 bytes, 58 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–52. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard 1* (teacher guide, Grade 3) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 803,827 bytes, 62 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–56. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series from Book 1 (M1.10 onwards). TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–15; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 16–45. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series (M1.16 onwards). The detailed sections of this guide are markedly thinner than in Books 1–2 (several state “Specific Content: N/A”); placements rest on the outcomes as stated. ## Australia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Australia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 books, all of them student textbooks** (Years 8–10: eight Cambridge volumes — the Essential Mathematics Australian Curriculum, Victorian Curriculum and CambridgeMATHS NSW lines, plus the GOLD support edition and the CORE pathway volume — and one Jacaranda volume, Maths Quest 10+10A) — and placed 844 teaching items. Australia has no government textbook series (ACARA writes the Australian Curriculum but publishes and prescribes no textbooks); the set is the school-used commercial series the country records name, and every book is cited as such. All 9 files are distinct works (distinct ISBNs, extents and contents, verified per book on the read); the publisher-line relations are stated in the placements: Book A1 (GOLD) is the support edition of Book A2's Year 8 material, Book A3 is the same line in its Victorian ©2024 edition, Books A4 and A6 are the NSW syllabus editions, and Book A9 is Book A8's CORE pathway companion. In the sections below the books are cited as Books A1–A9. Australia COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every item is pupil-textbook teaching. The read's four flagged claims are carried exactly: (F1) Book A3's contents lists its Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the file in hand contains the full chapter, bound after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941) — it is read and placed, and it founds this course's algorithmic-thinking lesson; (F2) Book A7's contents lists a Chapter 18, Algorithmic thinking, with no page numbers, and the chapter is NOT in the file (searched end to end; the book closes with its Glossary, printed p. 1106, and Index, printed p. 1117) — recorded as absent, not read, and no item rests on it; (F3) Book A9's running head at printed p. 644 prints section 10E's code as “0E” — the book's own production error; the placement cites 10E, as the section's heading and contents give it; (F4) only the 9 Maths books are this read's set — the country records' wider Australia picture (12 staged books across subjects, plus filled-by-exact-link titles) is not merged into this count, and in particular the two senior Maths titles that exist only as exact-link records (Cambridge Senior Specialist Mathematics, QCE Units 3&4, 2e, and Cambridge Senior Mathematical Methods, QCE Units 3&4, 2e) are NOT in hand and were NOT read. The placements' named exclusions — front matter and “how to use this book” guides; every chapter's pre-tests, progress quizzes, unlettered applications / problem-solving / modelling / investigation spreads, puzzles and games, chapter summaries, checklists and reviews, semester reviews, glossaries, indexes and answer sections — are apparatus/assessment, teach no new content, and are stocked nowhere. The set is Years 8–10 secondary only, so the primary lessons (counting, place value, first steps) receive no Australia item — that is the set's shape, not a reading gap. Australia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (9/9) has been read and placed; the two senior QCE exact-link titles do not block the in-hand class (if exact-link titles are ever ruled in-scope for the courses reckoning, Australia drops to PARTIAL until they are grabbed and read). Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items from Book A1, Book A2, Book A3.

**Book A1:** *Cambridge Mathematics 8 (CambridgeMaths GOLD)* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2012, ISBN 978-1-107-64383-3; 62,774,078 bytes, 554 PDF pp. The GOLD support edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line, developed for students who require additional support in mathematics; a distinct work from Book A2 (different ISBN, extent and arrangement), not a duplicate file. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 14).

**Book A2:** *Essential Mathematics for the Australian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2019, ISBN 978-1-108-77281-5; 35,833,730 bytes, 846 PDF pp. The main Year 8 Australian Curriculum volume of the Essential Mathematics line (Book A1 is its Gold support sibling). Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 18).

**Book A3:** *Essential Mathematics for the Victorian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2024, ISBN 978-1-009-48075-8; 24,871,876 bytes, 955 PDF pp. The Victorian Curriculum state edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line — a distinct work from Book A2. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 20). Its contents lists Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the full chapter IS bound into this file, after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941), and is read and placed (flag F1).

## Azerbaijan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Azerbaijan's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a pupil textbook: *Riyaziyyat – 2*, II hissə (Part 2) of the simplified-programme edition for special schools (Ministry of Science and Education, qrif № 2022-002; Çaşıoğlu, Bakı 2022), in Azerbaijani. The book is a real pupil textbook, so every Azerbaijan row in this course is a pupil-textbook row and Azerbaijan counts in every lesson it stocks — but at simplified-programme depth: its items are cited as the simplified-programme teaching they are, never as mainstream Azerbaijani curriculum** — and placed 65 teaching items. Two set corrections are proved by the read and carried exactly. (1) The staged record files this book as the mainstream Azerbaijani-sector Grade 2 textbook; it is not — it is the special-schools simplified-programme edition, **Part 2 only**, covering numbers 13–20; simplified Part 1 (76 pp) was never staged and is not in hand, and the mainstream Grade 2 *Riyaziyyat* (portal ids 664/665; older whole-book uploads of 147/147/162 pp, all checked and ruled out by page count) is not in hand at all. (2) The file is NOT pusher-secured: phase3-push-state.json lists it as pushed and reconcile sweep #6 (2026-11-05) called it pusher-secured, but a full enumeration of all 9 releases of tensae-code/liqlearns-textbooks (~7,300 assets, via the authenticated GitHub API, 2026-11-10) found NO Azerbaijan Mathematics asset anywhere — the only Azerbaijan assets are Informatics G2 and Technology G2 (mid-size-books-2) and Biology G11 (mid-size-books-3); the book was read from its ministry-repository original (trims.edu.az), identified exactly by its staged record (107 pp, the title-page simplified-programme wording quoted in books.json, and the legacy font encoding). Every mainstream Azerbaijani Maths textbook (all grades) remains link-only. The placements' named exclusions — the State Anthem front matter (PDF p. 2), the contents page and pictogram legend (printed p. 4), and the cover, title page, imprint and free-issue back cover — are apparatus/record pages, teach no mathematics, and are stocked nowhere.
