## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items: 2 teacher evidence, 3 pupil teaching. Sweden's pupil items in this lesson are genuine pupil teaching, so Sweden is added to this lesson's acceptance count above and to the countries line; its teacher and curriculum rows here are evidence only and carry no count.

**Book 2:** *Räkna med Abakus – Övningsbok* (pupil exercise book) — Sweden, SPSM, 2017, best.nr 11602; 1,647,686 bytes, 58 PDF pp., MD5 d8b1209258a64d21b43b9f2172e47364. Pupil exercise book for the Japanese soroban abacus, produced in braille and print editions mainly for blind pupils; exercises carry their answers printed beside them (self-checking design). PUPIL TEACHING. Book error (flag F5): the print ISBN is misprinted “987-91-28-11602-2” — the PDF ISBN is correctly 978-91-28-11605-3; the error is the book's and the content is taught as printed.

**Book 3:** *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — Sweden, SPSM, art. 12800; 1,593,356 bytes, 4 PDF pp., MD5 09c5d0cdd084e777ab56394dd4c49ed4. A pupil game about exchanging Swedish money, cited by its printed pp. 1–4; filed in the records under “Verklighetsuppfattning”, not under Matematik — confirmed on reading as a pupil maths game. PUPIL TEACHING.

**Book 4:** *Räkna med Abakus – Handledning* (teacher guide) — Sweden, SPSM, 2017, art. 11600/11604; 11,921,933 bytes, 60 PDF pp., MD5 aecd2e237b2b59a439adf7ac8db21042. Teacher guide to the same soroban method as Book 2; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Book errors: the print ISBN is misprinted “987-91-28-11600-8” (flag F5; the PDF ISBN is correctly 978-91-28-11604-6), and on printed p. 45 the division chapter says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division (flag F7) — taught corrected on the division row it touches. Its exercise sets (printed pp. 51–53) and maths-card copying masters (printed pp. 55–56) are apparatus — record item R2, stocked nowhere.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Subtraction without exchange and with five-exchange | Sweden — *Räkna med Abakus – Övningsbok* (pupil exercise book) — pupil teaching, printed pp. 23–35 |
| Subtraction with ten-exchange, with combined and complicated exchanges, and multi-row subtraction; ranges to 1,000 | Sweden — *Räkna med Abakus – Övningsbok* (pupil exercise book) — pupil teaching, printed pp. 23–35 |
| Subtraction play: played down from 100 kr (Växelspelet) | Sweden — *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — pupil teaching, printed pp. 1–4 |
| Worked subtraction algorithms on the abacus, e.g. 74−53, 357−34, 236−117, 134−68, 446−248 | Sweden — *Räkna med Abakus – Handledning* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only, printed pp. 28–33 |
| Written mental arithmetic, subtraction: two worked methods for 72−56 | Sweden — *Räkna med Abakus – Handledning* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only, printed pp. 34–35 |

Sweden enters this lesson as pupil teaching on the rows marked pupil teaching above — the Övningsbok and Växelspelet are genuine pupil materials (SPSM special-needs publications) — and is counted in the acceptance count above on those pupil rows alone; every other Sweden row in this lesson is teacher or curriculum evidence and is counted nowhere. Sweden's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-heavy): the in-hand set is read and stocked in full, but there is no mainstream pupil textbook in hand — Sweden has no state pupil textbook series, and its pupil side here is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils only.

Placements: liqlearns-courses-work/expansion-2026-11-10/maths/sweden-placements.md.

## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 11 items (10 curriculum evidence and 1 teacher evidence) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 4 and Book 5. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 1:** *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 1* (curriculum brochure) — Switzerland, CIIP (Conférence intercantonale de l'instruction publique de la Suisse romande et du Tessin), Plan d'études romand (PER), Version 3.0, août 2024; 899,121 bytes, 76 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 13–21) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 2* (curriculum brochure) — Switzerland, CIIP (Conférence intercantonale de l'instruction publique de la Suisse romande et du Tessin), Plan d'études romand (PER), Version 3.0, août 2024; 1,209,759 bytes, 116 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 14–29) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 4:** *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (cantonal curriculum) — Switzerland, Repubblica e Cantone Ticino, Dipartimento dell'educazione, della cultura e dello sport (DECS), edizione perfezionata 2022; 2,789,910 bytes, 266 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Matematica chapter (printed pp. 132–138) is placed here; every other chapter of the volume is another subject, and the chapter's process/attitude competence targets (MAT.I.7–9, MAT.II.9–11 and 13–16, MAT.III.9–20) carry no subject content and are stocked nowhere (MAT.III.14 restates the tool-use target placed once at item 330). CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the Piano states the competence targets pupils must reach and teaches nothing itself; its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 5:** *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (teacher guide) — Switzerland, © MaMa – DECS – Divisione della scuola (CeRDD), 2021; 16,669,707 bytes, 102 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed p. = PDF p. Read in full, cover to bibliography; §§1 and 3 (project history, platform, community) are apparatus, stocked nowhere, and §2's quoted Ticino Piano targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach numbers and calculation; it is not a pupil textbook, and its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

Switzerland enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — Switzerland has no pupil Maths textbook in hand; its in-hand set is three curriculum brochures, the mathematics chapters of two all-subjects curriculum volumes and one teacher guide, which state requirements and teaching guidance without being pupil textbooks (the standing evidence-only precedent). Switzerland's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the whole in-hand set is read and stocked, and it advances only when a Swiss pupil Maths textbook — one of the prescribed commercial series, today licence-gated — lands in hand and is read.

## Timor-Leste added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Timor-Leste's whole in-hand library from the PDFs — **5 files**, of which **2 carry mathematics**: the Grade 5 pupil exercise book *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* and the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) of *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2*, the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume. The other three files were checked in full and carry no maths teaching: *Livru Dalan Tetun ba Klase 5* (a Tetum grammar book — number words appear once, as one of eight grammatical word-classes), *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 6 Períodu 2* (Natural and Social Science plans only; its four “matemátika” mentions sit inside one density lesson) and *Um Mundo de Histórias 6º ano* (a Portuguese reader; maths appears only inside stories). The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher” split counts only the books filed as Maths and repeats a filing error — **flag F1** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/timor-leste.md` filing exists): the Grade 3 volume is filed as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide”, claiming Natural Science plans from p. 10 and Social Science plans from p. 468, but its own Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components and puts Matemátika at p. 318, Siénsia Naturál at p. 512 and Siénsia Sosiál at p. 624 — the mis-filing hid the country's only in-hand Maths teacher evidence. **Flag F2** (record gap): `textbooks.tsv` carries only 3 Timor-Leste rows — the two Grade 5 books, including the Maths exercise book, sit in `books.json`, the push state and the GitHub repo tree with no TSV row. Everything else Timor-Leste Maths is link-only and not in hand — the Matemática G1–G4 student books, the G10–G12 student manuals, the 2024 new-curriculum Grade 7 set and the Learning Passport book courses — and is cited nowhere. The placements number **177 items: 83 pupil-workbook items and 94 teacher-evidence items** (33 from full printed lesson plans; 61 from the period plan table alone, each marked “(plan table only)”). The workbook is a genuine pupil teaching book — 77 numbered exercise sets (Ezersísiu 1–77) plus two front-matter reading tables — but it practises and tests; it does not present topics first (its letter to teachers states the exercises follow the annual plan and lesson-plan sequence), and it is not the main Grade 5 textbook. The guide's Matemátika section is **Period 2 only** — the Period 1 and Period 3 volumes are not in hand, so Grade 3 coverage here is one period of three. The books' own errors are taught corrected: **E1** — the workbook's census table heads its second column “2005”, but its figures are the 2010 census and its questions ask about 2010; **E2** — Ezersísiu 70 is a reprint of Ezersísiu 68, counted once (as item 70); **E3** — Ezersísiu 60's fourth prism has equal dimensions in the Tetum text and different dimensions in the Portuguese (the Tetum governs); **E4** — the guide's Lisaun 51 plan tells the teacher to write “6 x 1 = 1” (read 6 x 1 = 6, as the page's own explanation gives); **E5** — the guide's introduction states 2 formal formative assessments per period while the plan schedules 6 (assessments are exclusions either way); **E6** — ten blank plan-table content cells are continuation/test rows, folded into their parent items, and lesson 84's body settles it as the fraction–decimal relation lesson. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbook's covers, imprint, forewords, letters and “do not write in this book” notice; the guide's discipline introduction and assessment guidance (printed pp. 326–328); its 22 consolidation lessons, 6 formal-assessment lessons and 5 project lessons (no new content stated for any of them); blank continuation/test rows; homework and in-lesson revision steps; and the Grade 3 volume's other eight subject sections. Counting grain: pupil book — one item per distinct practised skill; teacher guide — one item per distinct taught content in the period plan. Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — a pupil-workbook set on the Peru pattern: the workbook's rows count, the guide's rows are teacher evidence and count nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items: 1 pupil-workbook + 4 teacher evidence.

**Book 1:** *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* (pupil workbook — the Grade 5 Mathematics exercise book) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; bilingual Tetum/Portuguese; based on the 2014 National Basic Education Curriculum (Cycles 1–2), 1st edition May 2016, print run 38,900; 1,997,219 bytes, 70 imposed sheets (140 half-pages), MD5 44cd93b05692baaa17cc58569dc9cc5b. Complete as filed: Ezersísiu 1–77 all present between the front cover/imprint/forewords block and the back cover. **No folio is printed anywhere in the book** — the PDF is a printer's imposition, two portrait book pages per landscape sheet — so its items are cited by exercise number (Ezersísiu N). PUPIL WORKBOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure (the Peru workbook pattern).

**Book 2:** *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2* (teacher guide — the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume; its Matemátika section only) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; 15,881,272 bytes, 829 PDF pp., MD5 18b0a4e0d55af5f56868c556dc9893f2. Its Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components; the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) was read in full — full 50-minute lesson plans (objective, materials, timed teaching steps; the MAT3.x codes are the national curriculum's Grade 3 outcomes) plus the period plan table at printed pp. 318–325. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure, and the section is Period 2 only. Filed in the library records as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide” — the filing error F1 carried in the intro above.

Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** (pupil-workbook set, Peru pattern). Flags F1 (the Grade 3 volume's mis-filing) and F2 (the missing Grade 5 `textbooks.tsv` rows) are library-record matters — reported here, not fixed by this merge.

## Turkmenistan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Turkmenistan's whole filed in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 pupil files = 3 pupil works, plus 1 teacher guide** (6 documents, 840 PDF pages in all) — and the expansion map's "3 student + 0 teacher" kind split is **wrong** (**flag F1**: the map counts one file per grade/track slot; it misses the second parts of both Turkmen works and the teacher guide entirely — any plan sized on "3 books" under-sizes the read by about 2.5×). The pupil works are the complete Turkmen-medium Grade 1 and Grade 2 courses — *Matematika* Parts 1 and 2 for each grade (Atabaýew & Öwezowa, 3rd editions, 2019/2020) — and Part 1 only of the Russian-medium Grade 1 course (Rulieva, Chernikova, Mavlyanova & Satdykova, 4th ed. 2022), a separate work by a separate author team, not a translation of the Turkmen pair; the two Grade 1 Turkmen books are scans with no text layer and were read from page renders. The one teacher book, N. Açilowa's *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* (Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes, Ylym, 2015), is a teacher-training study guide: it teaches NO pupil content, so its rows are **teacher evidence only** and count in no lesson's taught-in figure; the five pupil books are real pupil textbooks, recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan and served by the Ministry's own E-kitaphana, so their rows **count**. **Flag F2** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/turkmenistan.md` filing exists): a `books.json` note claims the Russian Grade 1 Part 2 "is E-kitaphana post 507" — post 507 is in fact **Grade 2 Russian Mathematics Part 1** (per the enumeration table and the grid match), no Grade 1 Russian Part 2 post exists in the catalogue, and that Part 2 is recorded nowhere as found; post 507 must not be double-counted as both a Grade 1 and a Grade 2 book, and the Russian Grade 1 work stands in hand as Part 1 only. **Flag F3**: the filing notes for these books rested on samples ("every-fifth-page verification", contents lists not yet verified); this full re-read confirms the sampled claims spot-checked — e.g. the kilogram section of Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 2 does sit at printed pp. 63–64 — and supplies the missing contents lists for both Grade 2 parts (Part 1 contents at PDF p. 103; Part 2 at PDF pp. 118–119); no sampled content claim checked was found false. **Flag F4**: the map's "2 link-only" figure cannot be confirmed and looks far too low — the banked E-kitaphana grid match carries Maths links for the Grade 2 Russian pair and for Grades 3–9 Mathematics, Algebra and Geometry, plus a Grade 5 methodology guide; every one of those is link-only, not in hand and cited nowhere, and everything beyond Grade 2 is outside this set. Byte-identical duplicate posts are cited once, on the filed copy (post 403 = the Grade 1 Part 2 file; post 412 = the Grade 2 Part 1 file; post 418 = the Grade 2 Part 2 file; post 576 = the teacher guide's post 555 file), and every pupil file was pulled from its GitHub release asset at the exact recorded byte size (reconcile sweep #8 lists 23/23 Turkmenistan entries secured). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a book (its contents-list section, or a headed teaching block where the contents list runs broader), with consolidation/revision sections counted once per book-part; pure exercise pages inside a section are apparatus of that section's item. In every book in this set the printed folio equals the PDF page. One reading note, carried exactly: in the Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 1 book's type, the empty answer box prints as a small square — in the PDF font layer it can appear as «*» (e.g. the comparing exercises "7 * 6" mean "put <, > or = between 7 and 6") — it is NOT a multiplication sign, and multiplication is not taught in that book. Not stocked as separate rows, exactly as the placements direct: the Grade 2 Part 1 money item 14 (manat notes and coins inside addition/subtraction stories at printed pp. 50, 61 — context only, no money section is taught in that book, and the placements count it inside items 11–13), and the four consolidation/revision items the placements assign to no lesson (Grade 2 Part 1 item 17, Grade 2 Part 2 item 42, Grade 1 Part 1 item 71, Grade 1 Russian Part 1 item 125) — practice over sections already stocked, no new content. Turkmenistan's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** on the as-filed set. Into this lesson the placements stock 30 items: 29 pupil + 1 teacher evidence.

The book was grabbed byte-exact through the authenticated GitHub API (9,205,715 bytes, 184 PDF pages, MD5 d017ae08ae7c35d335757b0fb1ef747f — matching the library's verified record for the complete Wayback capture of 502107P00; staging holds no Tunisia PDF on disk) and read in full from rendered pages — the PDF's text layer is glyph-encoded (extraction is mojibake) and Arabic OCR returned nothing on these watermarked scans; the diagonal CNP watermark does not hide body text. Record items R1–R5 are apparatus or exam evidence, stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the assessment typology — diagnostic before new learning, formative during, certifying at a period's or year's end (printed pp. 41–42); R2 the six period evaluations (printed pp. 134–152) — exam evidence only; R3 the closing Arabic–French glossary of mathematical terms (printed pp. 154–157); R4 the guide's statement (printed p. 158) that the pupil textbook is held to at most 120 pages, so the extra integration and problem-solving situations are printed in this guide instead — the pupil book and this guide are designed as one package; R5 the bibliography (printed pp. 180–182). The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Trinidad and Tobago's whole in-hand Maths-bearing set from the files — **9 files, all curriculum/teacher evidence, 0 pupil textbooks**: the dedicated *Primary School Curriculum — Curriculum Guides: Mathematics (Infant 1 – Standard 5)* (MoE, Curriculum Planning and Development Division, 2013), read in full; seven per-level all-subject Primary Curriculum Guides (Infants 1, Infants 2, Standards 1–5), recorded in every record under the plain “Curriculum Guide” label, whose Mathematics sections are the **same curriculum text** as the dedicated guide's section for that level — verified file by file (normalised-text sequence similarity 0.9973 Infants 1, 0.9977 Infants 2, 0.9975 Standard 2, 0.9986 Standard 5; word-multiset containment 1.0000 Standard 1 and 0.9948 Standard 4, whose per-level productions extract in a different column order; Standard 3's scanned guide verified by OCR — rationale “What Is Mathematics About?” at PDF p. 60, “MATHEMATICS: STANDARD 3” tables at PDF pp. 70/80/90, three content spot-checks matching) — so they are **cited once, through the dedicated guide** (placements Record items R1–R7), never double-counted; and the *National Early Childhood Care and Education Curriculum Guide* (ages 3–4), whose mathematics strand is placed separately (items 245–249) because the dedicated guide starts at Infant 1. The expansion map's set picture (0 student + 1 teacher) is wrong as a set description: the dedicated guide it counted is real, but the true in-hand set is these 9 files, and **no pupil Maths textbook for Trinidad and Tobago exists in hand under any label** (all pupil textbooks are commercial — STP Caribbean Mathematics and others; none is held). Every document in the set is a Ministry curriculum guide — a document that states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself — so every Trinidad and Tobago item is a curriculum REQUIREMENT item, worded as curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per numbered Outcome in the guide's OUTCOMES column, with the placements' stated folds (a bare “solve problems involving X” outcome that restates its own section is folded into that section's item; per-measure vocabulary outcomes are folded into their measure's item; each strand's closing Language outcome is its own item). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the dedicated guide's front matter (PDF pp. 1–26 — contents, National Curriculum Framework prose, Subject Rationale); the Dispositions columns of every table (attitudes and values — apparatus, never items); the per-level guides' non-Mathematics subjects; the 17 Forms 1–3 guides staged at grade-08 (ELA, ICT, Spanish, Integrated Science, Social Sciences, VAPA, Visual Arts, Dance, Drama, Music, PE, HFLE, Technology Education — checked book by book; none is a Mathematics book; the Social Sciences staging anchor was labelled “Teacher's Guide” in its source bundle, but the file itself is the Social Sciences Curriculum Guide — a records mislabel, noted, not a Maths item); and the ECCE guide's non-mathematics strands and apparatus-level mentions (§5.9.9 equipment list; §5.9.16 a number line 1–10 in a materials list). Book errors are read corrected: in Standard 4's Number Patterns table the Outcomes-column wording of Outcome 5 is displaced across Outcomes 4 and 6 in print — the requirement was read from its elaborations (the items 183/184 boundary) and is placed corrected; a Standard 4 Decimals conversion elaboration cross-cites code [1.1.5] where [1.1.15] is meant, read as 1.1.15; and fraction glyphs (halves, quarters, fifths, tenths) are dropped at several points in the dedicated guide's text layer — the fractions were read from context and the surrounding elaborations. Set boundary: the *NCSE Mathematics Curriculum Guide (Forms 1–3)* is **link-only, not in hand** — exact links known (official MoE blob copy 2,900,122 B; IBE/UNESCO mirror 2,587,043 B) but the file was never staged or downloaded, so it was not read and is cited nowhere; the NCSE Mathematics *Teacher's* Guide has no public PDF on any route checked by the library fronts (provisional negative). Unit starts cited are the contents printed pages, verified against the folios. Verified COMPLETE (contents checked against pages; the book ends with its tests/examinations).

Zimbabwe enters this lesson as teacher-guide evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Zimbabwe's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook at all, only teacher's guides (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Zimbabwe's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand Maths book has been read and placed, and it advances only when a genuine Zimbabwe pupil Maths book lands in hand and is read — all 63 primary Learner's Book rows and every secondary Maths Learner's Book row in the library grid remain unfilled (publisher shop-only; ZIMSAKE copies are proven samplers; Scribd copies are reader-only leads).
